This page includes deck lists from players who recorded up to 17 points in the 10 Standard rounds. To see lists of players recording 18 or more points, [click here](https://web.archive.org/web/20100221000000/http://archive.wizards.com/magic/magazine/article.aspx?x=mtg/daily/eventcoverage/ptsd10/topstddecks).
